Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [112] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRAX，一个基于JAX和MJX的高效安全强化学习基准，相比CPU基准加速约100倍，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，实验发现无单一方法在所有任务中占优，并验证了课程学习与安全迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL基准因使用高保真3D物理模拟而计算缓慢，难以支持大规模实验和快速原型开发，亟需加速方案。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎，利用JAX的向量化操作和硬件加速构建CRAX基准，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体特定任务（各含3个难度级别），并评估6种主流安全RL方法。

Result: CRAX相比CPU基准加速约100倍；6种安全RL方法中无单一方法在所有任务上占优，存在性能与安全性的权衡；课程学习和安全迁移在困难设置下优于直接训练。

Conclusion: CRAX可作为高效的安全RL研究平台，揭示了现有方法的性能-安全权衡，并表明课程学习与迁移学习是提升困难场景下安全RL效果的有效策略。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [113] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）中客户端级和节点级两种粒度的模态不平衡问题，提出FedMGS框架。该框架通过隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成范式，包含可用性感知图编码器、原型引导潜在语义合成器及可靠性校准语义融合机制三个核心组件，在四个任务上实现了最高17.41%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-FGL面临两种粒度的模态不平衡挑战：客户端级（某些客户端完全缺失某种模态）和节点级（单个节点缺失视觉或文本属性）。现有研究主要针对图无关或集中式场景，难以直接适应联邦图学习环境。需要一种能在表示空间中直接恢复缺失模态语义的方法。

Method: 将模态不平衡MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知潜在语义表示合成问题，提出FedMGS框架，包含：1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；2) 原型引导潜在语义合成器，建立跨客户端语义锚点以合成缺失模态；3) 可靠性校准语义融合机制，在预测读出前调节恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上的大量实验表明，FedMGS持续优于竞品基线，最高提升达17.41%，且实现了最佳效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: FedMGS通过隐式语义合成范式有效解决了MM-FGL中的模态不平衡问题，为多模态联邦图学习提供了高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [114] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无需人工标注数据的意图分类合成对话框架，通过两种方式引入风格多样性（生成时属性控制与后处理风格迁移），结合LLM过滤，达到人类标注数据93.3%的性能，并发现风格多样性比主题多样性更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在快节奏工业场景中，人工标注的种子数据通常不可用，而现有合成数据方法依赖少量人工标注，因此需要一种完全无需人工标注的高质量合成数据生成方法。

Method: 1）利用两种主题和风格属性提升数据多样性；2）提出Univ和Exam两种后处理风格迁移模型，将LLM生成的合成话语转换为更丰富、更类似人类的语言风格；3）使用LLM作为评判器进行数据质量过滤。

Result: 在工业数据集和公开数据集上，该方法达到了使用人工标注数据训练性能的93.3%。

Conclusion: 风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键，可防止模型学习虚假的风格相关性；在生成过程中引入风格属性比后处理风格适应更有效。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [115] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计（DAE）方法，使其适用于部分可观测环境，并通过引入离散潜在动力学模型降低计算复杂度，在Arcade Learning Environment上验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAE方法在全可观测环境下表现优异，但存在两个局限：依赖完全环境可观测性限制了在现实场景中的应用，且建模转移概率需要大量计算开销，尤其在高维观测下成本过高。

Method: 1) 将DAE的理论框架扩展到部分可观测领域，仅需最小修改即可适配POMDP；2) 引入离散潜在动力学模型来高效近似转移概率，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上，DAE方法能够有效随函数逼近器容量扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了DAE的两个关键局限（部分可观测性与计算复杂度），使其在实际场景中更具实用价值。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [116] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向网络物理系统(CPS)中虚假数据注入攻击(FDIA)的轻量级防御框架，通过在输入样本中添加基于数据统计分布生成的伪特征填充，随机化输入维度，使对抗攻击因不可迁移性和填充结构不可预测性而变得计算不可行，无需修改核心DNN架构即可提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN在CPS中检测FDIA表现出高精度，但CPS独特的基础设施使DNN易被攻击者利用以规避检测，且传统防御机制难以应对CPS环境下FDIA的挑战。

Method: 提出一种模型无关的防御框架，通过额外的输入层对输入样本进行填充，填充值来自输入数据的统计分布（伪特征值）。该方法以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度，使对抗扰动由于不可迁移和填充结构不可预测而难以有效构造。

Result: 在IEEE 14母线、30母线、118母线和300母线系统上的电力系统状态估计任务中，该方法显著提升了模型鲁棒性，对性能影响可忽略不计，有效缓解了绕过传统防御的攻击。

Conclusion: 提出的填充策略是一种轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心架构的防御方法，在真实CPS环境中易于部署，能有效增强DNN对FDIA的抵抗能力。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [117] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战扩散模型中时间步嵌入的必要性，通过理论和实验证明，在特定条件下，去除显式时间条件后模型仍能保持甚至超越原有性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，探究U-Net和Diffusion Transformer架构在无时间条件下的表现。

Method: 理论证明在某些条件下扩散训练目标的全局最小值可以在无显式时间条件的情况下实现；在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融实验，完全移除时间步嵌入。

Result: 时间无关模型在FID、精确率和召回率等指标上可与条件模型媲美甚至超越；证明了架构可以从带噪输入中隐式推断噪声尺度。

Conclusion: 显式时间条件化是冗余的，这一发现挑战了长期以来的时间条件化范式，为更高效、聚焦于结构的生成架构铺平道路。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [118] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种为微分方程逆问题设计的神经网络代理模型，能够提供不确定性感知的预测，并通过延迟接受MCMC进行诊断，在高维参数空间中比高斯过程代理更高效，同时可融入微分方程约束，实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微分方程逆问题的数值方法计算成本高，尤其在贝叶斯框架下，复杂正向模型和高维参数空间中的似然评估极为昂贵，亟需一种计算高效且具有不确定性量化能力的代理方法。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络代理模型，用于替代微分方程求解器；利用延迟接受MCMC的短运行作为诊断工具，评估代理诱导的后验近似的保真度。

Result: DeepGaLA在前向模型近似精度上与高斯过程代理相当，但在高维参数空间中效率更优；能够融入包括非线性在内的微分方程约束。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题提供可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [119] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文密集型智能体（agent）工作负载，研究了4-bit KV缓存压缩技术。作者提出了UltraQuant方法，结合TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，以vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点。主要贡献包括：构建了多轮智能体场景下任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量的联合评估框架；描述了4-bit路径的实用设计选择（非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除、块级缩放变体）；在AMD GPU上实现了优化（包括优化的解码注意力内核和UltraQuant的FP4近似路径）。实验结果显示，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中，P50首个Token延迟降低3.47倍（所有轮次平均2.3倍），输出吞吐量比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体工作负载中，长前缀被多个短轮次复用，并发性决定GPU利用率，导致KV缓存面临巨大压力。现有FP8 KV缓存方案在压缩效率上仍有不足，亟需更高效的4-bit压缩方案来提升服务吞吐量和降低延迟。

Method: 采用4-bit KV缓存压缩，基于TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化；引入非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、移除QJL、块级缩放变体等设计；提出UltraQuant FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放和CDNA4原生缩放MFMA支持。

Result: 在长上下文多轮智能体工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中，P50首个Token延迟降低3.47倍（所有轮次平均2.3倍），输出吞吐量比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4-bit KV缓存压缩在上下文密集型智能体场景中显著优于FP8基线，UltraQuant方法有效平衡了任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量，特别是在AMD GPU上通过硬件原生支持实现了高效部署。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [120] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可控的玩具世界框架，利用生成器-分离器流程合成数据，研究持续学习中的遗忘机制，发现特征叠加并不必然导致遗忘，而是与表示强度和容量分配相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集纠缠了太多因素，导致很难隔离观察持续学习系统中知识遗忘的具体机制，因此需要一个可控框架来使这些机制可观测、可测试。

Method: 构建合成生成器-分离器流程，定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务；引入表示强度和叠加（特征间方向重叠）的可测量量；通过SINDy拟合保留率、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系；基于有效秩进行任务级分析。

Result: (1) 叠加随时间增加，但在任务边界出现瞬态下降；(2) 高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但不必然导致遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增加。

Conclusion: 叠加与遗忘之间的关系并非简单正相关，而是与表示强度、容量分配等因素相互作用；该玩具分析为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [121] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contagion Networks（传染网络）框架，用于量化大规模语言模型作为评估者时，其评估偏差如何在多智能体系统中传播。通过3个DeepSeek-chat智能体的实验，发现偏差传播系数γ在[0.157, 0.352]之间，同质模型间的传染系数比跨模型弱3-5倍，将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体系统中作为评估者时，其系统性的评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播，但缺乏正式的框架来量化这种偏差传播的程度和机制。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks框架，定义Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_N来量化偏差传播。在3个DeepSeek-chat智能体实验中使用三种不同的评估偏差配置文件（结构化、平衡、基于证据），通过谱半径ρ(Gamma_N)识别传播机制。

Result: 偏差传播系数γ在[0.157, 0.352]之间；同质模型智能体的传染系数比先前工作中跨模型系数弱3-5倍；将评估委员会从k=1增加到k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 同质模型的偏差传播处于抑制区（suppression regime），而增加评估委员会规模是一种有效的缓解策略。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [122] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINNs训练中因高度非凸损失函数导致的收敛不稳定、训练平台期及超参数敏感问题，提出了一种两阶段进化算法方法：第一阶段用低保真截断训练快速筛选超参数配置，第二阶段对最优候选进行完整梯度优化，在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz方程上显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs训练存在收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感的问题，其物理信息损失函数具有高度非凸和多目标结构，导致外环超参数搜索成为嘈杂的黑箱优化问题，传统局部或梯度方法易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出两阶段进化算法方法：阶段一进行低保真截断训练（使用较少epoch）快速筛选候选超参数配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外环问题；阶段二对最优候选进行标准梯度优化器下的完整训练以精细化求解。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个经典方程上，该方法在固定计算预算内一致优于标准训练，实现了显著更低的平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法凭借其种群探索能力和处理混合不可微搜索空间的鲁棒性，结合两阶段探索-利用策略，能有效提升PINNs的求解精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [123] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究DiffusionGemma这一扩散语言模型的推理透明度，将其分解为变量透明度和算法透明度两个维度。尽管扩散模型在连续潜空间中执行大量计算导致初始不透明，但通过可解释的token瓶颈将中间状态映射后，其不透明深度从Gemma 4的28.6倍降至1.1倍。然而算法透明度因扩散过程中所有token可同时变化而更具挑战性，案例研究揭示了非时序推理、token/序列模糊化等新现象。在可监控性方面，DiffusionGemma与Gemma 4表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理透明度对于理解模型决策、减轻误用和调试异常行为至关重要。扩散语言模型在连续潜空间中进行大量计算，这可能降低其推理透明度，因此需要系统评估这种新型架构的透明度特性。

Method: 将透明度分解为变量透明度（理解中间计算状态）和算法透明度（重建推理过程）两个维度，通过token瓶颈映射将扩散模型的中间状态转化为可解释表示，并进行一系列可解释性案例研究。

Result: 通过token瓶颈映射，DiffusionGemma的不透明深度从Gemma 4的28.6倍降至1.1倍；发现了非时序推理、token/序列模糊化、中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象；在可监控性上与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的透明度挑战主要在于算法层面而非变量层面，其变量透明度可通过适当映射达到与自回归模型相近水平，但算法透明度需要新的分析工具来理解扩散特有的分布式推理机制。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


### [124] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了偏倚缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保交叉性子组的充分代表性，并通过整数线性规划在偏倚近似误差与数据效率之间进行权衡，同时量化公平性的代价。实验表明该方法在保持预测精度的同时满足覆盖约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叉点处表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，这源于缺乏量化（交叉性）偏倚的原则性指标以及交叉性子组在训练数据中的代表性不足。

Method: 扩展偏倚缓解框架以纳入覆盖约束；将偏倚缓解建模为整数线性规划，优化所有缓解策略；通过允许小的近似偏倚误差来提高数据效率。

Result: 在公开数据集上，该方法在多个分类器下保持了预测准确性；覆盖约束对于保持下游ML性能至关重要；量化了公平性容忍度与数据修改成本之间的关系。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在偏倚缓解与数据效率之间实现可量化的权衡，覆盖约束对维持下游性能不可或缺，为法律合规和数据治理提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [125] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASYS是一个将偏微分方程求解转化为可测试可微符号程序的自动化框架，结合演化搜索与梯度优化，在多个非线性PDE问题上发现了先前未知的闭式解析近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表格理解PDE解，但传统分析需手工逐个处理，数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产生这些结构，因此需要自动化发现解析符号表示的框架。

Method: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架：将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的可微符号程序，通过演化搜索优化数学形式，同时用梯度优化拟合连续参数，实现自动化归纳偏置注入而非盲目符号回归。

Result: 在五个涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的问题上，ASYS产生了可解释的表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，此前这些场景没有闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式可能性，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似，有望为数学家提供进一步分析的引导。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [126] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fisher信息矩阵诱导的黎曼几何，定义了重参数化不变的黎曼锐度，严格证明了随机梯度下降隐式偏好平坦极小值且平坦极小值泛化更好的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧氏平坦度（如Hessian的迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数的重参数化下不具有不变性，这破坏了“SGD隐式偏好平坦极小值且平坦极小值泛化更好”这一直觉的理论基础。Dinh等人的工作对此提出了尖锐批评，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 将平坦性建立在Fisher信息矩阵诱导的统计流形黎曼几何上，定义黎曼锐度并证明其在光滑、保函数重参数化下的不变性；将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为协方差与FIM成比例的结构，推导SDE的平稳分布，证明概率质量指数级集中于黎曼平坦极小值；通过PAC-Bayes泛化界将几何偏置与测试性能联系起来。

Result: 黎曼锐度在光滑保函数重参数化下严格不变（真实FIM），经验对角估计量仅近似继承不变性，精确不变性需K-FAC等结构化估计量；SGD的平稳分布指数级集中于黎曼平坦极小值；PAC-Bayes界由黎曼锐度显式控制；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，SR可靠追踪泛化性能，且其随η/B的缩放符合理论预测，而欧氏锐度无法做到。

Conclusion: 本文提供了关于平坦极小值为何泛化更好的严格、重参数化不变的理论解释，弥合了直觉与理论批评之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [127] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边际优势累积（MAA）机制，通过跨批次操作级证据累积解决批量式痕迹蒸馏中反馈矛盾的问题，在4个基准和4个目标模型的16项设置中取得14项最佳结果，并将优化阶段token消耗降低约75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量式痕迹蒸馏中，同一内存操作在不同批次间可能收到矛盾的反馈信号，现有方法缺乏跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中操作。

Method: 形式化定义了可对齐性和可比性两个结构条件；提出边际优势累积（MAA）方法，通过构建差分信号实现跨批次可比性，利用指数移动平均为每个操作累积带符号证据，并通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16项设置中，MAA在14项上取得最佳结果，持续超越现有批量级蒸馏基线，在大多数设置中匹配或超越在线替代方法，同时将优化阶段token消耗减少约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了批量式痕迹蒸馏中的反馈矛盾问题，在提升性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [128] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种针对低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的图绑定检查点与恢复机制。FlashRT是基于此的运行时系统，在RTX 5090上可实现亚毫秒级的GPU快照与恢复，TTFT加速比在16k token下达到27倍，且胶囊保存了包括KV缓存、循环状态、卷积状态等在内的完整执行边界状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要通过分页或基数KV缓存来复用前缀工作，这种方法在高吞吐、高并发场景下非常有效，但仅管理执行状态的单个位置片段（KV缓存）。本文研究的是低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务场景（如交互式LLM代理、语音系统、机器人策略），这些场景需要频繁地分支、重置、中断和重新进入，且响应预算紧张，需要一种更完整的执行状态复用机制。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules）——一种图绑定的检查点与恢复机制，用于在提交边界处捕获完整的可恢复状态。FlashRT是一个白盒、面向后端的核运行时系统，其评估的NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续的静态缓冲区内运行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于活跃状态是一组封闭的命名缓冲区，胶囊可以快照、恢复、分叉或回滚整个执行边界，包括KV状态、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据。该方法将复用从以token为地址的KV片段转移到以图边界为单位的执行状态边界。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别上是字节精确的，在贪婪解码下是token一致的。仅KV的消融实验出现分歧，表明循环状态是承载负载的关键。GPU驻留快照和恢复为亚毫秒级，TTFT加速比从2k token时的3.9倍增长到16k token时的27倍。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上，相同的正确性和结构属性成立。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊并非替代高吞吐KV缓存服务，而是定义了一个互补的、以延迟优先的服务点，用于显式的执行状态复用，特别适用于需要频繁分支、重置和中断的低延迟物理AI服务场景。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [129] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推断。该框架将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，通过Transformer在训练时学习跨先验族进行预测自适应，在分布偏移场景下匹配贝叶斯最优预测器且速度快几个数量级，并在真实时空温度预测任务中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯预测推断虽能提供不确定性量化和鲁棒泛化，但精确推断难以处理，现有近似方法要么计算昂贵，要么需要限制性建模假设导致预测性能下降。已有的先验数据拟合和上下文模型与训练先验的支持紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时适应新先验的显式机制，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架，将先验信息作为上下文数据集的前缀显式表示；训练Transformer模型在序列化的先验任务和目标任务上进行学习，使其能够在不同先验族之间自适应调整预测。

Result: 在一系列难度递增的评估任务上（包括元分布外先验和高维潜结构先验），该方法匹配了贝叶斯最优预测器，同时速度提升数个数量级；在真实时空温度预测基准上也验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 多任务上下文学习能够有效摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推断，在保持贝叶斯最优性的同时大幅提升计算效率，并增强对先验分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [130] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于可预测性的隐私"（Privacy via Predictability）框架，该框架通过量化攻击者在观察到算法输出后对敏感信息的预测能力增益来衡量隐私泄露，与差分隐私（DP）在理论上不可比较，但可在特定条件下建立联系，并提供了基于GMM的渐近分析方法及相应的输出扰动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）在最坏情况下的隐私-精度权衡代价高昂，缺乏针对特定攻击者知识和特定敏感信息的细粒度度量。本文旨在提出一种更精细的隐私框架，显式整合攻击者的先验知识（受损数据），以提供比DP更灵活、更针对具体场景的隐私保障。

Method: 提出"可预测性"（Predictability）作为隐私度量，衡量攻击者观察到算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息预测能力的增量增益。利用广义矩估计（GMM）框架分析在平稳、遍历、混合过程生成受损数据下的渐近可预测性，并基于此推导出针对ERM的可预测性校准输出扰动方案。

Result: 证明可预测性与差分隐私在一般情况下不可比较（各自可以很小而另一个很大）。但在所有个体中仅有一人未被攻陷且所有二元查询均被视为敏感信息的最坏情况下，可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私（mutual-information DP）。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了一种比DP更细粒度的隐私度量，能够针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型进行定制。该方法与DP互补，可与DP结合使用以实现细粒度的隐私控制。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [131] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出李代数注意力（Lie-Algebra Attention），将注意力机制中的token定义为矩阵李群元素（裸变换），利用群元素的相对姿态的对数范数作为闭式注意力分数，无需表示论工具或学习核函数，可处理包括仿射全帧群在内的任意矩阵李群，并在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的序列补全实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量token的注意力方法在处理群结构时，要么依赖不可约表示（irrep）或满射指数映射（surjective-exp），无法覆盖包含尺度和剪切的非紧非交换仿射群；要么需要学习核函数，参数效率低。作者希望构造一种直接以群元素为token的注意力机制，无需表示论工具，天然满足群等变性和不变性，并能处理任意矩阵李群。

Method: 将token定义为矩阵李群元素$g_i$（裸变换，无特征负载）。相对几何为$g_i^{-1}g_j$，其对数$\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)$作为配对不变量的闭式表达式。注意力分数$s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$，即块加权Frobenius内积下的规范邻近核，完全闭式，无需学习核函数、不可约表示、球谐函数或Clebsch-Gordan积。该方法适用于任意矩阵李群，包括非紧非交换的仿射群。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的序列补全实验中，闭式分数匹配甚至超越（SE(2)上）学习型MLP核，使用的分数参数减少50-80倍；同时向量token基线方法的等变性被破坏了5到12个数量级。

Conclusion: 李代数注意力提供了一种简洁、高效的群等变注意力机制，无需表示论复杂工具即可处理包括仿射群在内的任意矩阵李群，在保持等变性的同时大幅降低参数开销。

Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [A land of monotone plenty, bis repetita: from classical to weak optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19516)
*Virginie Ehrlacher,Rodrigue Lelotte,Luca Nenna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了经典最优运输中的c-循环单调性本质上等价于零阶最优性条件（即扰动成本非负），并将该视角推广到弱最优运输问题，其中该性质对应于一阶最优性条件（线性化成本非负）。在适当假设下，两种情形下该性质均能刻画最优运输计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在从最优性条件的角度重新理解c-循环单调性这一经典概念，并将其推广到弱最优运输问题中，填补该领域缺乏相应单调性刻画的理论空白。

Method: 通过将最优运输问题的最优性等价于径向扰动锥上线性运输成本泛函的非负性，将c-循环单调性重述为零阶最优性条件；进而对弱最优运输成本泛函进行线性化，将相应单调性解释为一阶最优性条件。

Result: 建立了经典c-循环单调性与零阶最优性条件的等价关系，并成功将其推广至弱最优运输情形，得到对应的一阶最优性条件；在适当假设下，证明了该性质对最优运输计划的刻画能力，经典结果得到统一处理且证明得到简化。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了经典与弱最优运输问题中单调性概念的理论框架，揭示了二者分别对应不同阶数的最优性条件，为后续研究提供了新的分析视角和工具。

Abstract: The celebrated c-cyclical monotonicity property is shown to boil down to the zeroth-order optimality condition for the optimal transport problem. More precisely, we show that optimality is equivalent to the non-negativity of the linear transport cost functional on the radial cone of admissible perturbations. We then utilise this point of view to extend the c-cyclical monotonicity property to the weak optimal transport problem, for which it corresponds to the first-order optimality condition, namely to the non-negativity of the linearisation of the weak transport cost functional near the optimiser. Altogether, this sheds new light on this monotonicity concept. For both classical and weak optimal transport, we show that this property characterises (under suitable assumptions) optimal transport plans. In the classical case, we recover known results of the literature but with revisited proofs.

</details>


### [133] [On the Limits of Biased Derivative Information for Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19553)
*Anant Shyam,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有有偏梯度（包括随机和有偏）的平滑非凸优化中寻找δ-驻点的问题。针对一阶算法给出了紧的下界，并建立了依赖偏差的高阶算法下界。提出了信赖域方法和高阶方差缩减方案，在某些偏差范围内匹配下界，并展示了高阶导数信息在偏差较大时的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸优化研究多假设梯度无偏（随机噪声），但实际场景中梯度往往存在系统性偏差（如模型压缩、对手攻击、模拟偏差等）。作者旨在揭示偏差对优化复杂度的影响，并探索高阶信息是否能在有偏场景下突破无偏随机优化的已知限制。

Method: 1) 构建一阶算法的紧下界，证明达到O((ε+B²)^{1/2})-驻点所需的复杂度，匹配已有上界。2) 建立依赖偏差的高阶算法下界，目标为O(ε+B_max)-驻点。3) 设计信赖域方法，在特定偏差范围内实现下界匹配。4) 提出高阶方差缩减方案，在高偏差设置中降低预言机复杂度。

Result: 一阶情形下给出与现有上界匹配的紧下界；高阶情形下给出偏差依赖的下界；信赖域方法在特定偏差范围内匹配下界；高阶方差缩减方案在高偏差场景下显著降低复杂度，且证明了在无偏随机场景中无法实现此类改进。

Conclusion: 在存在梯度偏差的非凸优化中，一阶算法的复杂度紧界已明确；高阶信息在偏差足够大时能带来实质性加速（超越无偏随机场景的理论限制），但依赖偏差的具体量级。这为设计实际有偏优化算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding $δ$-stationary points for $δ> 0$, i.e., $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $||\nabla F(x)|| \le δ$, for smooth, non-convex objectives, where the derivative oracles are not only stochastic but also biased. In the first-order setting, we provide tight lower bounds for finding an $O((ε+ B^2)^{1/2})$-stationary point, for $ε> 0$ and where $B$ is a bound on the gradient bias, matching the upper bounds of Ajalloeian and Stich (2020). We then establish bias-dependent lower bounds for algorithms that use higher-order derivative information for finding $O(ε+ B_{\max})$-stationary points, where $B_{\max}$ is a bound on the maximum bias for all derivatives. To complement these lower bounds, we develop trust-region based methods that, for certain ranges of bias, provide guarantees that match the corresponding lower bounds. We further improve upon the oracle complexity in high bias settings through a higher-order variance reduction scheme, in particular demonstrating the benefits, in some cases, of using higher-order derivative information, whereas such improvements are known to be unattainable for stochastic unbiased settings.

</details>


### [134] [Mean-Field Control with a Common Hidden State under Decentralized Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19639)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ali D. Kara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究多决策者共享隐藏状态、通过相同信道获取分散观测的系统最优控制问题。在无限智能体极限下，问题简化为单一智能体控制问题，其中智能体通过动作的条件分布影响隐藏状态动态。证明了随机化对最优性的必要性，但混合策略非必需；无限种群的最优对称策略对有限种群是近似最优的，收敛速率为$1/\sqrt{N}$，随策略记忆长度指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多决策者在共享隐藏状态和分散观测下的最优控制问题，特别是当系统代价仅依赖于智能体动作的经验分布时，如何从无限智能体极限简化问题并建立可解的理论框架。

Method: 将无限智能体极限问题转化为确定性测度值控制问题，在策略空间上建立动态规划递归。分析了随机化策略的必要性，以及对称策略的最优性。通过收敛速率分析建立无限种群与有限种群之间的联系。

Result: 随机化控制动作对最优性必要，但混合策略非必要；无限种群最优对称策略对有限种群近似最优，收敛速率$1/\sqrt{N}$，随策略记忆长度指数增长。

Conclusion: 建立了多智能体分散观测控制问题的极限理论框架，证明了无限与有限种群之间的近似最优性关系及其收敛速率。

Abstract: We study optimal control of a system with multiple decision makers who share a common hidden state and receive fully decentralized observations through identical channels. The dynamics of the hidden state and the cost incurred by the agents depend on the agents' actions only through their empirical distribution. In the limit problem with infinitely many agents, the problem reduces to a single agent control problem where the agent affects the hidden state dynamics via the conditional law of the actions given the past values of the hidden state process. We formulate this problem as a deterministic measure valued control problem over the space of policies and provide a dynamic programming recursion.
  We first show that for the limiting problem randomization over the control actions is necessary for optimality. However, randomization over the selection of policies (i.e., mixture policies) is not required. We then show that the optimal symmetric policies designed for the infinite population problem are near optimal for the finite population problem.
  In particular, we establish convergence rates that decay with number of agents as $\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}$, and grow exponentially with the memory length used in the policy.

</details>


### [135] [Counterexample to a conjecture on the pairwise independent correlation gap using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19663)
*Arjun Ramachandra,Karthik Natarajan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文借助AI工具GPT5.5 Pro，对Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）提出的两两独立相关间隙猜想的反例进行了构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Ramachandra和Natarajan（2025）关于两两独立相关间隙的猜想，展示其不成立。

Method: 利用AI工具GPT5.5 Pro辅助构造反例，通过具体的数学实例来证伪该猜想。

Result: 成功构造出一个反例，表明Ramachandra和Natarajan的猜想并非普遍成立。

Conclusion: 两两独立相关间隙猜想存在反例，需要进一步修正或重新审视。

Abstract: Aided by the AI tool GPT5.5 Pro, we provide a counterexample to a conjecture made by Ramachandra and Natarajan (2025) [Pairwise independent correlation gap, Operations Research Letters, 107255, 6040].

</details>


### [136] [Learning Neural Maximal Lyapunov Functions on $\mathsf{SO}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19669)
*Adeel Akhtar,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov框架，用于学习特殊正交群SO(n)上动力系统的最大Lyapunov函数，并通过Zubov型特征刻画最大吸引域，理论证明了逼近能力，导出了对数映射导数的显式可计算公式，并通过两阶段算法实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 李群上的动力系统稳定性分析是基本挑战，因为经典的欧几里得空间Lyapunov方法无法直接应用于弯曲几何结构，需要专门针对李群几何特性的稳定性分析框架。

Method: 提出基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov架构，证明其逼近能力；利用Zubov型特征刻画最大吸引域；推导对数映射导数的显式数值可计算公式；设计两阶段训练算法平衡计算效率与精度。

Result: 在低维非线性系统上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效地为SO(n)上的动力系统学习最大Lyapunov函数，为李群上系统的稳定性分析提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Establishing stability guarantees for dynamical systems on Lie groups is a fundamental challenge, as classical Lyapunov methods developed for Euclidean spaces do not directly transfer to curved geometries. In this paper, we propose a framework for learning maximal Lyapunov functions for systems evolving on the special orthogonal group $\mathsf{SO}(n)$. Theoretically, we introduce a neural Lyapunov architecture based on the logarithmic map with proven approximation capabilities, and we formulate the learning problem via a Zubov-type characterization of the maximal region of attraction. A key technical contribution is the derivation of explicit, numerically tractable formulas for the derivative of the logarithmic map, enabling training through a two-phase algorithm that balances computational efficiency and accuracy. Empirically, we validate the approach on a low-dimensional nonlinear system.

</details>


### [137] [Stochastic Representations of Stationary HJBI-Type Variational Inequalities with Bilateral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19705)
*Sheng Huang,Qingmeng Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带双边约束的稳态HJBI型变分不等式的概率表示，提出了两种互补的随机表示：一种通过增广无限时域两人零和随机微分博弈（引入两个额外停时符号将障碍项纳入运行收益）；另一种通过两人零和混合控制-停时随机微分博弈（每个玩家同时选择连续控制和停时决策，基于具有随机终端时间的BSDE定义收益）。证明了相应值函数是双边HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为带双边约束的稳态HJBI型变分不等式提供概率表示，建立其与随机微分博弈之间的严格联系。

Method: 1) 增广无限时域两人零和随机微分博弈：通过扩大控制空间（引入两个额外停时符号）将障碍项嵌入运行收益，利用无限时域随机递归微分博弈框架证明值函数为粘性解；2) 混合控制-停时博弈：每个玩家选择连续控制和停时决策，通过具有随机终端时间的BSDE定义收益，引入依赖于对手控制过程的非预期停时策略以适配Elliott-Kalton策略框架，基于惩罚化无限时域SDG及其值函数、动态规划论证和向后半群稳定性估计进行证明。

Result: 两种表示得到的下值和上值函数都是对应HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解；混合控制-停时博弈的值函数与双边HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解一致。

Conclusion: 提供了两种互补的概率框架来表示带双边约束的稳态HJBI变分不等式，建立了变分不等式与随机微分博弈之间的严格理论联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we study probabilistic representations for stationary HJBI-type variational inequalities with bilateral constraints. We provide two complementary stochastic representations.The first representation is obtained through an augmented infinite-horizon two-player zero-sum stochastic differential game (SDG). By enlarging the control spaces with two additional stopping symbols, the obstacle terms are incorporated into the running payoff. Using the framework of infinite-horizon stochastic recursive differential games, we show that the resulting lower and upper value functions are the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the corresponding HJBI variational inequalities. The second representation is given by a two-player zero-sum mixed control--stopping SDG. In this formulation, each player chooses both a continuous control and a stopping decision, and the payoff is defined by a BSDE with a random terminal time. To make the stopping component compatible with the Elliott--Kalton strategy framework, we introduce nonanticipative stopping strategies depending on the opponent's control process. The proof is based on penalized infinite-horizon SDGs coupled with their own value functions, together with dynamic programming arguments and stability estimates for backward semigroups. We prove that the value functions of the mixed control--stopping game coincide with the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the bilateral HJBI variational inequalities.

</details>


### [138] [Signature Methods for Optimal Market Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19772)
*Alberto Gennaro,Thibaut Mastrolia,Francesca Primavera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于特征的签名方法（Sig-REINFORCE算法）来解决均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题，通过签名线性化技术将问题简化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化，并在泊松过程和自激霍克斯过程两种场景下进行了测试，与PPO基线进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在均值-方差准则下求解最优做市问题，传统的动态规划方法难以处理，需要开发新的高效计算方法。

Method: 采用签名线性化技术，将做市问题转化为对增强市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化；提出Sig-REINFORCE算法来学习最优买卖报价。

Result: 在泊松过程和自激霍克斯过程两种市场订单到达场景下进行了测试，并以PPO算法作为基线进行了对比。

Conclusion: 基于签名的Sig-REINFORCE算法能有效求解均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题。

Abstract: We propose a signature-based method to solve the optimal market-making problem under a mean-variance criterion. By exploiting signature linearization techniques, we reduce the market-making problem to a pseudo-linear optimization over the expected signature of an augmented market path, and we develop a signature algorithm named Sig-REINFORCE to learn the optimal bid and ask quotes. We test our method in two scenarios, in which market-order arrivals follow either a Poisson or a self-exciting Hawkes process, and we benchmark it against a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) baseline.

</details>


### [139] [Dynamic Core Allocation for Malleable Jobs with Unknown Speed-up Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19789)
*S. ~A. Bodas,J. ~L. Dorsman,M. Mandjes,L. Ravner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究多核计算系统中可塑作业的动态资源分配问题，通过迭代学习和控制框架，在未知加速比参数下最小化系统平均作业数（即平均响应时间）。提出基于最大似然估计的参数估计方法和两种结合动态规划的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核系统中可塑作业的并行度可动态调整，但作业类别的加速比函数参数未知，导致难以最优分配核心资源以最小化平均响应时间。

Method: 构建迭代学习-控制框架：交替执行（1）基于状态相关离去时间的最大似然估计器估计未知加速比参数（证明强一致性）；（2）求解对应MDP更新核心分配策略（类内均分，类间按MDP比例分配）。提出两种结合估计与动态规划策略更新的学习算法。

Result: 证明了固定分配策略下最大似然估计的强一致性；通过数值实验验证了两种学习算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能在加速比参数未知时有效学习最优核心分配策略，降低系统平均响应时间。

Abstract: We study dynamic resource allocation in a multicore computing system with a fixed number of processing cores and a stream of {\it malleable} jobs. Each job may adjust its level of parallelism during execution, allowing adaptive redistribution of resources across concurrently active jobs. Jobs belong to one of two observable classes, each characterized by a distinct speed-up function with unknown parameters. The objective is to learn a core-allocation policy that minimizes the long-run mean number of jobs in the system, equivalently the mean response time in steady state.
  \noindent To address this uncertainty, we develop an iterative learning-and-control framework. The system alternates between estimating the unknown speed-up parameters from observed job completions and solving the associated Markov decision process (MDP) to update the allocation policy. Within each job class, cores are shared equally among active jobs; the fraction of capacity assigned to each class is obtained from the MDP formulation of \cite{berg2017}, evaluated at the current parameter estimates. We construct a maximum likelihood estimator based on state-dependent inter-departure times and prove its strong consistency under a fixed allocation policy. We further propose two learning algorithms that combine this estimation step with dynamic programming-based policy updates, and illustrate their through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [140] [Semiglobal Input-Delay Tolerance Algorithm for Distributed Nonconvex Optimization of Networked Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19871)
*Jing-Zhe Xu,Zhi-Wei Liu,Ming-Feng Ge,Yan-Wu Wang,Dinxin He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对存在输入时滞和一致性约束的网络化非线性系统，提出了输入时滞容忍半全局收敛（IDTSC）概念，并开发了半全局输入时滞容忍（SIDT）算法，在输入时滞与非线性动力学耦合下实现了分布式优化，并通过Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件将适用性扩展至非凸优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法多假设系统可忽略输入时滞或仅考虑线性系统，但在网络化非线性系统中，输入时滞与非线性动力学的耦合会严重破坏优化收敛性和一致性约束的满足。本文旨在解决这一尚未充分研究的挑战性问题。

Method: 采用层次化设计框架，结合输入-状态稳定性（ISS）分析，构建SIDT算法；通过定义IDTSC概念（对任意预紧致初始集存在可容许时滞上界），保证在该界内节点状态收敛至最优解并满足一致性约束；利用Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件放宽严格凸性假设，拓展至非凸优化。

Result: 理论上证明了SIDT算法在输入时滞下可实现IDTSC；通过Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件将算法适用范围从强凸优化扩展至更一般的非凸优化；数值实验验证了算法在网络化非线性系统输入时滞场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为输入时滞下的网络化非线性系统分布式优化提供了半全局收敛保证，所提SIDT算法兼具时滞鲁棒性和非线性适应性，且非凸拓展显著增强了实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of distributed optimization problems in networked nonlinear systems (NNSs) subject to input delays and consensus constraints. It introduces input-delay tolerant semiglobal convergence (IDTSC), meaning that for any prescribed compact initial set there exists an admissible delay bound under which the optimal solution is computed within consensus constraints and all node states converge to the solution. Building on a hierarchical design and input-to-state stability analysis, a new semiglobal input-delay tolerant (SIDT) algorithm is developed that practically achieves IDTSC for distributed optimization under the coupling between input delays and nonlinear dynamics. Further, by relaxing strict convexity requirements through the Polyak-Łojasiewicz condition, the SIDT algorithm broadens its applicability to nonconvex optimization. Finally, numerical experiments corroborate the theory on NNSs with input delays.

</details>


### [141] [A Regularized Nikaido-Isoda Function Approach to Multi-Leader-Follower Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20013)
*Atsushi Hori,Takayuki Okuno,Ellen H. Fukuda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正则化Nikaido-Isoda函数的多领导-跟随博弈（MLFG）新重构方法，将其近似为单层可微纳什均衡问题，无需跟随者博弈的导数信息，也无需假设跟随者问题的凸性，从而适用于更广泛的MLFG类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MLFG求解方法（如EPEC或代入响应函数）存在可扩展性差、需要高阶导数信息等问题，难以处理大规模或非凸的跟随者博弈问题。

Method: 利用正则化Nikaido-Isoda函数，通过引入惩罚参数将MLFG近似为单层可微的纳什均衡问题，无需跟随者博弈的导数信息及凸性假设。

Result: 在全局子解析条件下，分析了原始MLFG均衡与所提重构问题均衡之间的数学关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法可处理更广泛的MLFG问题，且具有良好的理论保证。

Abstract: A multi-leader--follower game (MLFG) is a hierarchical noncooperative game in which leaders compete at the upper level while taking into account the followers' best responses at the lower level. A typical approach to solving the MLFG reformulates it as an equilibrium problem with equilibrium constraints (EPECs) by replacing the lower-level game with its KKT conditions. Another approach, when each follower's response is unique, is to reformulate the MLFG as a Nash equilibrium problem by substituting these response functions into each leader's problem. However, both reformulations may lack scalability since higher-order derivatives may be required when solving the resulting problems.
  In this paper, we propose a new reformulation of the MLFG by exploiting a regularized Nikaido--Isoda function and approximating the MLFG by a single-level differentiable Nash equilibrium problem with a penalty parameter. The proposed reformulation neither requires derivative information on the followers' game nor assumes convexity of each follower's problem; hence, it can handle a broader class of MLFGs. Under global subanalyticity, we analyze the mathematical relationship between equilibria of the original MLFG and the proposed reformulation.

</details>


### [142] [Robust $Q$-learning for mean-field control under Wasserstein uncertainty in common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20356)
*Mathieu Laurière,Ariel Neufeld,Kyunghyun Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Wasserstein不确定性下针对离散时间均场控制问题的鲁棒Q-learning算法，该算法结合量化投影方案和Wasserstein对偶重构，证明了同步和异步学习下的收敛性和有限时间迭代界，并通过数值实验验证了算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均场控制问题通常假设公共噪声的分布完全已知，但实际中该分布常存在不确定性（模型误设），亟需一种能够处理公共噪声律不确定性的鲁棒强化学习算法。

Method: 提出鲁棒Q-learning算法，核心创新包括：(1) 将量化投影方案与Wasserstein对偶重构相结合，处理公共噪声空间上的分布不确定性；(2) 分别设计了同步和异步两种学习方案；(3) 建立了算法收敛性的有限时间迭代界。

Result: 理论上证明了算法在同步和异步两种学习方案下的收敛性及有限时间迭代界；数值实验在系统风险和流行病模型上验证了算法有效性，展示了异步实现与理想化Bellman迭代的对比、在公共噪声误设下的鲁棒性-性能权衡，以及异步Q-learning的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒Q-learning算法能够有效处理均场控制中公共噪声律的不确定性，具有理论收敛保证和良好的实际性能表现。

Abstract: In this article, we present a robust $Q$-learning algorithm for discrete-time mean-field control problems under Wasserstein uncertainty in the common noise law. The algorithm combines a quantization-and-projection scheme with a Wasserstein dual reformulation on the common-noise space. We establish its convergence together with finite-time iteration bounds for both synchronous and asynchronous learning schemes. Numerical experiments on systemic risk and epidemic models compare the asynchronous implementation with an idealized Bellman iteration, illustrate the robustness-performance tradeoff under common-noise misspecification, and report the observed convergence behavior of the asynchronous $Q$-learning algorithm.

</details>


### [143] [Optimization with inequality constraints by the embedded gradient vector field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20059)
*Petre Birtea,Ioan Casu,Dan Comanescu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入二次松弛变量，为带不等式约束的优化问题建立了黎曼几何框架，将可行集提升为光滑子流形，利用嵌入梯度向量场方法推导出拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式，重新诠释了KKT条件，并给出了二阶最优性条件及互补松弛条件的几何对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不等式约束优化问题依赖拉格朗日乘子法和KKT条件，缺乏直观的几何解释。本文旨在通过黎曼几何语言为这类问题提供统一的几何框架，揭示约束优化问题的内在几何结构。

Method: 引入二次松弛变量将不等式约束转化为等式约束，并将可行集提升为扩展空间中的光滑子流形；分析约束流形的分层结构；利用嵌入梯度向量场形式推导拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；通过每个层上的限制Hessian矩阵推导二阶最优性条件。

Result: 得到了拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；重新推导了经典KKT一阶必要条件；获得了二阶最优性条件；识别出拉格朗日乘子符号条件作为经典互补松弛条件的几何对应；在双冰激凌锥实例中验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何框架为不等式约束优化问题提供了全新的视角，将经典优化理论与黎曼几何联系起来，具有理论上的统一性和推广潜力。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for constrained optimization problems with inequality constraints through the introduction of quadratic slack variables. This formulation makes it possible to employ the language of Riemannian geometry and to solve the problem via the embedded gradient vector field method. We lift the feasible set to a smooth submanifold of an extended ambient space. The stratified structure of the resulting constraint manifold is analyzed in detail, yielding a natural partition according to which constraints are active. Using the embedded gradient vector field formalism, we derive explicit, determinantal formulas for the Lagrange multiplier functions directly from the geometry of the constraint manifold, recovering and re-framing the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker first-order necessary conditions without invoking the classical Lagrange multiplier method. Second-order optimality conditions are obtained by computing the restricted Hessian on each stratum, and a complete sign condition on the Lagrange multipliers is identified as the geometric counterpart of the classical complementary slackness condition. The theory is illustrated on the double ice-cream cone example, where the geometry of the problem determines the nature and number of local minima.

</details>


### [144] [Optimal Coarse Correlated Equilibria in Mean Field Games: Linear Programming and No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20062)
*Luciano Campi,Federico Cannerozzi,Ioannis Tzouanas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场博弈，提出了最优粗相关均衡的概念，建立了线性规划公式，证明了最优LP粗相关均衡的存在性，并设计了基于无遗憾原始-对偶算法的学习方法，给出了收敛率证明和数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续时间平均场博弈中，粗相关均衡作为一种比纳什均衡更宽松的均衡概念，能够在个体理性约束下实现更好的系统性能。然而，如何在所有粗相关均衡中选择一个优化给定性能指标（可能不同于个体目标）的均衡，以及如何高效地学习这样的均衡，是尚未解决的问题。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为一个优化问题，然后建立线性规划（LP）公式来描述粗相关均衡，证明LP与原始概率设定之间的等价性，并证明最优解的存在性。在此基础上，利用外部遗憾约束的拉格朗日形式，设计了一个无遗憾的原始-对偶学习算法。

Result: 建立了连续时间平均场博弈中粗相关均衡的LP表征，证明了最优LP粗相关均衡的存在性，给出了所提学习算法的显式收敛率，并通过数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间平均场博弈中的粗相关均衡选择与学习提供了完整的理论框架和实用算法，其LP形式为实际计算提供了可行的路径，学习算法的收敛性保证了方法的可部署性。

Abstract: We introduce optimal coarse correlated equilibria for continuous-time mean field games. A coarse correlated equilibrium is a randomized recommendation scheme from which no player can gain by ignoring the recommendation and switching to an alternative strategy. The problem is as follows: a moderator selects, among all mean-field coarse correlated equilibria, one that optimizes a prescribed performance criterion, which may differ from the representative player's objective. After formulating the problem, we develop a linear programming (LP) formulation, prove the existence of optimal LP coarse correlated equilibria, and relate the LP characterization to the original probabilistic setting. Building on this characterization, we design a no-regret primal-dual algorithm, based on an equivalent Lagrangian formulation of the external-regret constraint, for learning such equilibria. We provide explicit convergence rates for the learning algorithm, and numerical examples illustrate the method.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Averaging in John Ellipsoid Approximation: High-Accuracy Algorithms in the Leverage-Score Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20082)
*Xiaoyu Li,Junwei Yu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文剖析了对称多面体John椭球的经典迭代算法，指出现代算法将三种成本（认证、识别、精度）混为一谈，其中$\varepsilon^{-1}$因子仅来自认证成本。通过D-最优设计对偶视角，证明均匀平均迭代的Frank-Wolfe间隙恰好为$\Theta(1/T)$，而使用热启动加速方法和阻尼牛顿法后，精度依赖降至双对数级别$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$，主要瓶颈转向识别成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离并精确定位John椭球/杠杆分数算法中$\varepsilon^{-1}$复杂度的真实来源，厘清认证、识别和精度三种成本，以突破长期以来认为该问题受限于$\varepsilon^{-1}$迭代的认知。

Method: 通过D-最优设计形式$\min_{\mathbf{p}\in\Delta_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$建立对偶字典，将杠杆分数预言机视为一阶预言机，$(1+\varepsilon)$-John保证等价于Frank-Wolfe间隙$g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$。分离三种成本后，对最后迭代使用热启动加速方法（$C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrt{\kappa}\log(1/\varepsilon))$查询）和阻尼牛顿法（$O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$查询）。

Result: $\varepsilon^{-1}$因子完全来自认证成本（均匀平均迭代的Frank-Wolfe间隙为$\Theta(1/T)$）；精度成本可降至双对数级别$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$；总查询数为$C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$，其中$C(\mathbf{A})$是$\varepsilon$-独立的设置成本。

Conclusion: 精度并非该问题的计算瓶颈；开放问题在于识别成本（到达最优面的无条件界）以及相应的下界。

Abstract: The John ellipsoid of a symmetric polytope $P=\{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^d:\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x}\|_\infty\le1\}$, $\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, is computed by a long line of leverage-score algorithms, from Cohen, Cousins, Lee and Yang (COLT 2019) to its successors [WY24, CLS+25], all reaching a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation in $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$ iterations. We separate this complexity into three costs the modern line conflates (certification, identification, and accuracy) and locate the historical $\varepsilon^{-1}$ in the first alone. In the equivalent D-optimal-design form $\min_{\mathbf{p}\inΔ_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$, the leverage-score oracle is exactly the first-order oracle and the $(1+\varepsilon)$-John guarantee the Frank-Wolfe gap $g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$; through this dictionary the costs come apart. The $\varepsilon^{-1}$ is a certification artifact: the uniform average of the iterates, the certificate used throughout the line, has gap exactly $Θ(1/T)$, however cheap each iteration is made. Pointed instead at the last iterate the same oracle is fast: a warm-started accelerated method reaches the guarantee in $C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries after an $\varepsilon$-independent setup $C(\mathbf{A})$, and once the optimal face is identified the facial problem is an unconstrained self-concordant minimization whose Hessian the oracle recovers exactly, so damped Newton needs only $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ steps, for a total of $C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries. The accuracy dependence is thus doubly logarithmic after an $\varepsilon$-independent, condition-dependent setup; the open problem is the remaining identification cost (a condition-free bound on reaching the optimal face) and lower bounds. Accuracy is not the obstruction.

</details>


### [146] [Optimizing Agricultural Drone Operations: From Launch and Recovery Siting to Tiered Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20239)
*Ethan Kolby,Josh Noble,Max Z. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向农业无人机喷洒作业的运营框架与基准分析方法，通过设施选址启发式算法和分层路径分解，将规划时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以内，同时仅牺牲4%的服务面积，实现分钟级规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机作业利润空间有限，现有优化工具随问题规模增大呈指数级增长，亟需实用的启发式方法支持日常运营规划。

Method: 采用p-Median启发式替代混合整数规划进行设施选址，并通过将目标区域划分为6-8个空间簇的分层问题分解方法进行路径规划。

Result: 设施选址方面，启发式方法将运行时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以下（降低三个数量级），服务面积仅减少4%；路径规划方面，分层分解方法将计算时间降低一个数量级，服务面积损失极小。

Conclusion: 该框架在商用硬件上实现分钟级规划，具备运营实用性；未来将融入天气建模、设施选址与路径规划的联合优化，并在更多田间几何形态下验证。

Abstract: Drones are increasingly used in agriculture, where tight margins demand efficient planning. Current optimization tools suffer from exponential runtimes as problem sizes grow, necessitating practical heuristics for daily operations. This paper presents an operational framework and benchmarking analysis for drone spraying operations. We evaluate the trade-offs between facility siting methods and tiered routing parameters. For facility siting, comparing a Mixed-Integer Program (MIP) baseline against a $p$-Median heuristic shows that the heuristic reduces runtime by three orders of magnitude, from over 97 seconds to under 1.2 seconds, with only a 4\% reduction in serviced field area. For route planning, a tiered problem decomposition approach partitioning the target area into 6 to 8 spatial clusters reduces computation time by an order of magnitude with minimal degradation in serviced area. This framework achieves minute-scale planning on commodity hardware, demonstrating operational relevance. Future research will incorporate weather modeling, integrated optimization of facility location and routing, and validation across diverse field geometries.

</details>


### [147] [Diagonal Hessian Approximation Based on Conjugacy Condition for Noisy Derivative-Free Optimization Problems in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20304)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie--Kafaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对大规模含噪无导数优化问题，本文提出一种基于对角近似共轭条件更新的进化策略方法，在噪声水平高、排序不可靠时比传统矩阵自适应方法更高效稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模含噪无导数优化中，矩阵自适应进化策略(MAES)在噪声水平高时因排序选择不可靠导致性能下降，尤其在高效维度下效率显著降低。

Method: 用对角近似代替全仿射缩放矩阵，基于共轭型条件，利用连续归一化重组位移进行保守对角更新，避免估计梯度/次梯度/插值模型或从含噪排序中学习稠密协方差信息。

Result: 在含噪基准测试中，所提方法与MAES型基线相比具有竞争力，且在噪声水平高、排序选择不可靠时通常更高效，同时计算成本低于全矩阵自适应和有限内存仿射缩放变体。

Conclusion: 提出的对角近似共轭条件更新方法在噪声环境下提供了稳定的缩放机制，是MAES类方法在高噪声场景下的有效替代方案。

Abstract: We consider large-scale noisy derivative-free optimization (DFO) problems in which only function values are available and gradient or subgradient information cannot be reliably estimated. Matrix-adaptation evolution strategies (MAES) and their limited-memory variants are among the most robust DFO methods under noise; however, their performance may deteriorate when the noise level is large. In such regimes, sorting and selection may misidentify informative sampled points, making the recombination step less reliable and weakening the scaling information used by affine or matrix-adaptation mechanisms. This can substantially reduce the efficiency of MAES-type methods, especially in high-dimensional settings.
  To address this limitation, we propose a DFO method that replaces the full affine-scaling matrix with a diagonal approximation constructed from conjugacy-type conditions. The proposed mechanism does not attempt to estimate gradients, subgradients, or interpolation models, nor does it learn dense covariance information from noisy rankings. Instead, it uses consecutive normalized recombination displacements in a conservative diagonal update, thereby limiting the influence of unreliable selection information while preserving the derivative-free structure of the underlying evolutionary framework. As a result, the method is computationally cheaper than full matrix-adaptation schemes and limited-memory affine-scaling variants, while providing a stable scaling mechanism in noisy environments. Numerical experiments on noisy benchmark problems show that the proposed method is competitive with, and often more efficient than, MAES-type baselines, particularly when the noise level is large and ranking-based selection becomes unreliable.

</details>


### [148] [A Single-Loop Minorized Dual Decomposition Method for Nonsmooth Multi-Stage Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20383)
*Dan Luo,Hailin Sun,Lei Yang,Yang You*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单循环最小化对偶分解方法，用于解决具有非光滑复合目标的多阶段随机规划问题。该方法利用阶段和场景结构，每次迭代构建最小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，然后执行一次对称Gauss-Seidel非精确ADMM来生成下一步迭代。该方法保持了阶段和场景的可分解结构，适合并行实现。建立了三阶段情形的全局收敛性，并推广到一般多阶段情形。数值实验验证了计算可行性和良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机规划问题具有内在的阶段和场景结构，现有方法在处理非光滑复合目标时效率有限，需要一种既能保持问题分解结构又具有良好收敛性质的并行化求解框架。

Method: 提出单循环最小化对偶分解方法：每次迭代构建一个最小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，然后在该对偶问题上执行一次对称Gauss-Seidel基于的非精确ADMM迭代来更新变量。该方法保持了阶段和场景的可分解结构，适合并行计算。

Result: 建立了三阶段情形的全局收敛性定理，并推广到一般多阶段情形。数值实验展示了该框架的计算可行性和良好的阶段-场景扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的单循环框架有效结合了最小化技术和ADMM，为非光滑复合多阶段随机规划提供了一种可并行、可扩展且具有收敛保证的求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-stage stochastic programming (MSP) problems with nonsmooth composite objectives. Tailored to their intrinsic stage-wise and scenario-wise structure, we develop a single-loop minorized dual decomposition method, in which each iteration constructs a minorized problem and its restricted Wolfe dual, and then performs \textit{one iteration} of the symmetric Gauss--Seidel based inexact alternating direction method of multipliers on the resulting dual problem to generate the next iterate. A key feature of the proposed optimization framework is that the resulting updates preserve the stage-wise and scenario-wise decomposable structure of the MSP problem and are suitable for parallel implementation. We establish global convergence of the generated iterates for the three-stage case and further establish the corresponding global convergence theorem for the general multi-stage setting. Numerical experiments illustrate the computational viability of the proposed framework and its favorable scaling behavior with respect to the stage-wise and scenario-wise structure.

</details>


### [149] [High-Probability Last-Iterate Guarantees for Two-Point Gaussian Zeroth-Order Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20446)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对光滑强凸随机优化问题，本文证明了标准同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法具有直接的高概率最后迭代收敛界 \( \widetilde{\mathcal O}(d/T) \)，且置信度依赖为对数形式而非多项式形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化理论通常仅提供期望意义上的收敛保证，或通过马尔可夫不等式从期望界转化得到高概率界（导致多项式置信度依赖）。本文旨在建立直接的高概率最后迭代收敛结果，避免上述间接转化带来的松弛，同时保持对数置信度依赖。

Method: 采用标准同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法：每轮抽取独立高斯方向，在相同随机样本下评估目标函数的两个对称扰动点，并执行范数归一化的随机逼近步。证明技术包括：(1) 高斯角度的均匀加权扫描；(2) 角度放大的乘积鞅边界，用于控制由展开随机递归产生的带符号后缀乘积项。

Result: 当维度满足 \(d \ge 16\log(6T/\delta)\) 时，以至少 \(1-\delta\) 的概率成立 \(f(\bx_T)-f(\bx^*) = \widetilde{\mathcal O}(d/T)\)，置信度依赖为 \(\log(1/\delta)\) 而非 \(1/\delta\) 的多项式。

Conclusion: 本文首次为同样本高斯零阶随机梯度方法在条件亚高斯随机梯度噪声下建立了直接的高概率最后迭代收敛界。该结果避免了对期望界的马尔可夫转化及噪声截断，且置信度依赖达到对数级别，显著优于间接方法的理论保证。

Abstract: We establish a direct high-probability last-iterate guarantee for the standard same-sample two-point Gaussian zeroth-order stochastic-gradient method applied to smooth, strongly convex stochastic optimization. At each iteration, the method draws a fresh Gaussian direction, evaluates the objective at two symmetric perturbations using the same stochastic sample, and takes a norm-normalized stochastic-approximation step. Assuming unbiased stochastic gradients and a conditional exponential-moment bound on the squared norm of the stochastic-gradient noise, we prove that, whenever \(d\ge16\log(6T/δ)\), \[ f(\bx_T)-f(\bx^*) =
